Recommendation: Execute only if guardrails pass; otherwise hold cash and wait for cleaner evidence.

In a broadly risk-on regime, some traders chase micro-cap squeezes and short-interest extremes. However, our framework explicitly prefers quality, margin of safety, and repeatable catalysts. The current macro environment does not require micro-cap speculation to capture upside; there are many higher-quality vehicles aligned with the same risk-on backdrop. SGN sits in the speculative tail of the distribution and is misaligned with the Chittick Cash lens and the strategy’s risk discipline.
